## SRC 08_MARKET_DATA/csv/peer_daily_closes_2021-2026-10-05.csv | data through 2026-10-05 | daily close price panel (CSV, 1488 rows x 22 tickers) | 2021-01-04 to 2026-10-05
- Raw file copied to CORE_NOTES/data/peer_daily_closes_2021-2026-10-05.csv (use it for any daily-level query). Columns: date (unnamed index col) + ADS.DE, ASO, BBY, BOOT, CROX, DECK, DKS, FIVE, FL, HIBB, JD.L, LULU, NKE, ONON, PUM.DE, SKX, SPY, TSCO, UAA, ULTA, WSM, XRT. Local-currency prices (JD.L in GBp, ADS.DE/PUM.DE in EUR, rest USD). Values carry float noise (e.g. DKS 2021-01-04 = 47.1694221496582), consistent with adjusted closes; file does not state adjustment basis. Rows = union of trading days across exchanges (blank where a market/ticker has no print).
- All figures below are [d] (computed by note-writer from the CSV; prices rounded to 2 dp, returns in %).
